| `\RQoneErrEdgesQwenRoundZero` | 1506 | results/wp6/20261023-215350-f5aad526/summary.md:90 \| Error analysis: missed precedence edges \| Every session replayed from `llm_cache.jsonl.gz` in replay mode (no LLM call): 640 of 640 round rows of `rounds.csv` reproduced (every score column). Edges: the true edges whose two activities the answer matches by name (those of edge recall), pooled over the sessions of a cell; recall = stated / edges. Side: the narrative states each dependency once, with its successor ("It can only start once A is done.") or with its predecessor ("It must be finished before C begins."); the structured style lists every activity's predecessors. Numbers: the stating block's duration in words or digits. d: paragraphs between the blocks of the two activities (structured: one paragraph per activity). Missed: edges not stated; on the predecessor side: their share stated with the predecessor (all: the share of all edges). Intervals: 95 % percentile intervals of a cluster bootstrap over instances (2000 resamples). \| row Backend=ollama, Style=narrative, Round=0 \| column "Edges" |
